# sub_ Rename Index

Machine-generated index of the bulk `sub_XXXXXX` -> semantic-name campaign.
The `sub_` name encodes the original virtual address and remains the canonical
anchor into `clash95.asm` / `clash95.map`; grep this file to map either
direction. Confidence follows `docs/REVERSE_ENGINEERING.md` section 2:
high = 2+ independent signals, medium = strongly suggested, low = honest
mechanical description only (gameplay role unproven).

| Original | Recovered name | Confidence | Area | Evidence |

| `sub_4A5000` | `Help_IndexTopicFile` | high | Help | Opens a text file via IO_FOpen, registers its name record via sub_4A5570, then scans lines for BEGIN-ENTRY-/END-ENTRY markers (aBeginEntry/aEndEntry), calling sub_4A5610 to parse each entry header and sub_4A57D0 to insert it into the topic tree; error strings 'Unmatched end marker.', 'Previous entry not closed.', 'File already loaded.', 'Could not open file' match CLIPS-style help-file loading. |
| `sub_4A52C0` | `Help_UnloadTopicFile` | high | Help | Looks up a file-name record in the dword_51ACE0 linked list, frees its topic tree via sub_4A5A80, unlinks the record from the list, and frees the 92-byte record with Mem_SmallBlockFree(v5, 92) - the inverse of Help_IndexTopicFile/Help_GetOrCreateFileRecord. |
| `sub_4A5350` | `Help_OpenFileAtNavigatedTopic` | medium | Help | Delegates topic lookup/advance to sub_4A5920 (a1 filename, a4 optional keyword) to get a file offset, then IO_FOpen's the help file and IO_SeekStreamGuarded's to that offset; called from Help_RunInteractiveHelpBrowser with a4=0 to step to the next topic entry. |
| `sub_4A53B0` | `Help_ReopenFileAtActiveEntry` | medium | Help | Finds the file record by name in dword_51ACE0, requires its stored 'current entry' pointer (offset +84) to be set (else error -25), then opens the file and seeks to the stored offset field of that entry (+88) - reopens the file positioned at the already-selected topic rather than navigating. |
| `sub_4A5460` | `Help_ReadEntryLine` | high | Help | fgets's one line from stream_handle; converts a leading '$$' escape to spaces; stops and closes the stream when the line matches aEndEntry (sub_4A54C0(buffer, aEndEntry) not found) or on EOF; used throughout the help browser to stream one topic's body text. |
| `sub_4A54C0` | `Str_FindSubstringIndex` | medium | Str | Generic substring search: scans a1 for the first position where a2 fully matches, returning that index or -1 if a1 is empty or no match found; used to test lines against aEndEntry/aBeginEntry markers in the help file loader. |
| `sub_4A5530` | `Str_UppercaseToDelimiter` | medium | Str | Walks a string uppercasing characters (toupper_) until IsTable[ch+1] < 0 flags a stop/delimiter character; used by Help_ParseEntryHeader and Help_FindOrAdvanceTopicEntry to normalize topic keywords for case-insensitive comparison. |
| `sub_4A5570` | `Help_GetOrCreateFileRecord` | high | Help | Searches the dword_51ACE0 linked list for a record matching filename a1 via strcmp_; if found returns it, else allocates a new 92-byte (0x5C) record, copies the name in, zeroes its tree/current fields, and appends it to the list - the registration counterpart of Help_UnloadTopicFile. |
| `sub_4A5610` | `Help_ParseEntryHeader` | high | Help | sscanf_(line, "%d%1s%12s%s", ...) parses entry number / type char ('M' menu or 'I' info) / keyword / delimiter; allocates a 104-byte (0x68) entry node, validates the delimiter equals aBeginEntry (else 'Invalid delimeter string.'/'Invalid entry type.' errors), records the file offset via ftell_, and uppercases the keyword via Str_UppercaseToDelimiter. |
| `sub_4A57D0` | `Help_InsertEntryIntoTopicTree` | high | Help | Links a freshly parsed entry (a2) into the topic tree under parent a1, ordering siblings by entry number via dword_51ACE4 ('current' cursor); rejects attaching subtopics to a non-menu entry with 'Line %d : Non-menu entries cannot have subtopics.' |
| `sub_4A5920` | `Help_FindOrAdvanceTopicEntry` | high | Help | Looks up the file record by name; if a4 (keyword) is given, searches the children of the current entry for a case-insensitive match via Str_UppercaseToDelimiter/Str_FindSubstringIndex; if a4 is null, advances the current pointer to the next sibling - dual find/advance topic navigation used by the interactive help browser. |
| `sub_4A5A80` | `Help_FreeTopicTree` | high | Help | Recursively frees a topic entry node tree: for each node frees children (i[23], the subtopic pointer) then Mem_SmallBlockFree(i, 104), walking the sibling chain - matches the 104-byte entry size from Help_ParseEntryHeader. |
| `sub_4A5AB0` | `Help_RunInteractiveHelpBrowser` | high | Help | Lazily loads the default help file (aClips_hlp = 'clips.hlp') printing 'Loading help file entries from ...Please wait...', registers/activates an IO router named aWhelp ('whelp') with query callback sub_4A6220, then loops opening topics starting at 'MAIN' (aMain_2), reading entry lines via Help_ReadEntryLine, and pausing with a 'press return'/'a for all' prompt via Lexer_PeekChar - a full CLIPS-style (help) command implementation. |
| `sub_4A5E30` | `Help_SetHelpFilePathCommand` | high | Help | Registered-style host function: if called with an argument, unloads the current help file (Help_UnloadTopicFile) and stores the new path from Lexer_ParseValueList (prints 'Releasing help entries...'); if called with no argument (Rules_RtnArgCount()==0), prints the current path (or aClips_hlp default) via 'The current help...' message (aTheCurrentHelp). |
| `sub_4A5FA0` | `Help_LoadHelpFileCommand` | high | Help | Host function: parses a filename argument via Lexer_ParseValueList, calls Help_IndexTopicFile (sub_4A5000) to load/index it, and returns the entry count via Rules_AddIntegerValue on success or reports 'Unable to load file...' (aUnableToLoadFi) plus the file-open error text on failure. |
| `sub_4A60A0` | `Help_PrintTopicEntryCommand` | medium | Help | Similar setup to Help_RunInteractiveHelpBrowser (uses sub_4A6270/sub_4A6550 to resolve a topic) but streams the entry's lines straight to an output logical name (redirecting 'T' to stdout) via Help_ReadEntryLine with no pause/prompt loop - a non-interactive 'print one topic' variant of the help browser. |
